This makes multiple changes in a single action collapse into a single re-evaluation.\n*/\n\ntype NodeKind = 'base' | 'derived'\ntype DependencyKind = 'state' | 'base' | 'selector'\n\ninterface Dependency {\n  path: string\n  kind: DependencyKind\n  value: any\n}\n\ninterface AtomicNode {\n  name: string\n  id: string\n  kind: NodeKind\n  state: AtomicLogicState\n\n  // base selectors\n  read?: (state: any, props: any) => any\n\n  // derived selectors\n  inputSelectors: Selector[]\n  inputNodes: (AtomicNode | undefined)[]\n  staticInputNames: string[]\n  computeFn?: (...args: any[]) => any\n  memoizeOptions?: Record<string, any>\n  selector?: Selector\n\n  dependencies: Map<string, Dependency>\n  dependents: Set<string>\n\n  evaluations: number\n  dirtyCause: string | null\n  dirty: boolean\n  stateInvalidated: boolean\n  evaluating: boolean\n  hasValue: boolean\n  value: any\n  lastInputs: any[]\n  lastProps: any\n}\n\ninterface AtomicLogicState {\n  logic: Logic\n  pathString: string\n  nodes: Map<string, AtomicNode>\n  order: string[]\n}\n\ninterface Collector {\n  node: AtomicNode\n  active: boolean\n  deps: Map<string, Dependency>\n  pending: Map<string, Dependency>\n}\n\nlet logicStates = new WeakMap<Logic, AtomicLogicState>()\nlet logicStatesByPath = new Map<string, AtomicLogicState>()\nlet selectorMeta = new WeakMap<Function, { state: AtomicLogicState; node: AtomicNode }>()\nlet crossDependents = new Map<AtomicNode, Set<AtomicNode>>()\nlet trackedProxyTargets = new WeakMap<object, any>()\nlet activeCollector: Collector | null = null\n\nexport const CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR = '[KEA] Circular dependency detected'\n\n/** Clears all engine state. Called when a new Kea context is opened. */\nexport function resetAtomicEngine(): void {\n  logicStates = new WeakMap()\n  logicStatesByPath = new Map()\n  selectorMeta = new WeakMap()\n  crossDependents = new Map()\n  trackedProxyTargets = new WeakMap()\n  activeCollector = null\n}\n\nfunction linkCrossDependency(from: AtomicNode, to: AtomicNode): void {\n  let dependents = crossDependents.get(from)\n  if (!dependents) {\n    dependents = new Set()\n    crossDependents.set(from, dependents)\n  }\n  dependents.add(to)\n}\n\nfunction foreignDependencyName(node: AtomicNode): string {\n  return `${node.state.pathString}::${node.name}`\n}\n\nexport function isAtomicSelectorsEnabled(): boolean {\n  const context = getContext()\n  return !!context && !!context.options && context.options.atomicSelectors === true\n}\n\nfunction getStateForLogic(logic: Logic): AtomicLogicState {\n  let state = logicStates.get(logic)\n  if (!state) {\n    state = {\n      logic,\n      pathString: logic.pathString,\n      nodes: new Map(),\n      order: [],\n    }\n    logicStates.set(logic, state)\n    logicStatesByPath.set(logic.pathString, state)\n  } else {\n    // The path may have been changed by a later build step. Keep the path index fresh.\n    state.pathString = logic.pathString\n    logicStatesByPath.set(logic.pathString, state)\n  }\n  return state\n}\n\nfunction getOrCreateNode(state: AtomicLogicState, name: string, kind: NodeKind): AtomicNode {\n  let node = state.nodes.get(name)\n  if (!node) {\n    node = {\n      name,\n      id: `${state.pathString}::${name}`,\n      kind,\n      state,\n      inputSelectors: [],\n      inputNodes: [],\n      staticInputNames: [],\n      dependencies: new Map(),\n      dependents: new Set(),\n      evaluations: 0,\n      dirtyCause: null,\n      dirty: false,\n      stateInvalidated: false,\n      evaluating: false,\n      hasValue: false,\n      value: undefined,\n      lastInputs: [],\n      lastProps: undefined,\n    }\n    state.nodes.set(name, node)\n    state.order.push(name)\n  } else {\n    node.kind = kind\n    node.id = `${state.pathString}::${name}`\n  }\n  return node\n}\n\nfunction createCollector(node: AtomicNode): Collector {\n  return {\n    node,\n    active: true,\n    deps: new Map(),\n    pending: new Map(),\n  }\n}\n\nfunction finalizeCollector(collector: Collector): Map<string, Dependency> {\n  collector.active = false\n  for (const [path, dependency] of collector.pending) {\n    if (!collector.deps.has(path)) {\n      collector.deps.set(path, dependency)\n    }\n  }\n  collector.pending.clear()\n  return collector.deps\n}\n\nfunction addDependency(collector: Collector, path: string, value: any, kind: DependencyKind): void {\n  if (!collector.active) {\n    return\n  }\n  collector.pending.delete(path)\n  collector.deps.set(path, { path, kind, value })\n}\n\nfunction addPendingDependency(collector: Collector, path: string, value: any, kind: DependencyKind): void {\n  if (!collector.active || collector.deps.has(path) || collector.pending.has(path)) {\n    return\n  }\n  collector.pending.set(path, { path, kind, value })\n}\n\nfunction resolvePendingDependency(collector: Collector, path: string): void {\n  if (!collector.active) {\n    return\n  }\n  collector.pending.delete(path)\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Tracking proxies\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\nfunction isTrackable(value: any): boolean {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') {\n    return false\n  }\n  if (Array.isArray(value) || value instanceof Map || value instanceof Set) {\n    return true\n  }\n  const proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(value)\n  return proto === Object.prototype || proto === null\n}\n\nfunction dependencyKey(key: any): string {\n  if (typeof key === 'string') {\n    return key\n  }\n  if (typeof key === 'symbol') {\n    return String(key)\n  }\n  try {\n    return String(key)\n  } catch (e) {\n    return String(JSON.stringify(key))\n  }\n}\n\nfunction childPath(path: string, key: PropertyKey): string {\n  return path ? `${path}.${String(key)}` : String(key)\n}\n\nfunction unwrapTrackedValue(value: any): any {\n  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {\n    const target = trackedProxyTargets.get(value)\n    if (typeof target !== 'undefined') {\n      return target\n    }\n  }\n  return value\n}\n\nfunction wrapTrackedValue(value: any, path: string, collector: Collector, pendingPath: string): any {\n  if (!isTrackable(value)) {\n    return value\n  }\n  if (value instanceof Map) {\n    return createTrackedMap(value, path, collector, pendingPath)\n  }\n  if (value instanceof Set) {\n    return createTrackedSet(value, path, collector, pendingPath)\n  }\n  return createTrackedObject(value, path, collector, pendingPath)\n}\n\nfunction createTrackedMap(target: Map<any, any>, path: string, collector: Collector, pendingPath: string): any {\n  const recordKey = (key: any, depValue?: any) => {\n    const keyPath = `${path}.map:${dependencyKey(key)}`\n    if (collector.active) {\n      resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n      addDependency(collector, keyPath, typeof depValue === 'undefined' ? target.get(key) : depValue, 'state')\n    }\n    return keyPath\n  }\n\n  const proxy = new Proxy(target, {\n    get(map, prop, receiver) {\n      if (prop === 'get') {\n        return (key: any) => {\n          recordKey(key)\n          return map.get(key)\n        }\n      }\n      if (prop === 'has') {\n        return (key: any) => {\n          const keyPath = recordKey(key, map.has(key))\n          return map.has(key)\n        }\n      }\n      if (prop === 'keys' || prop === 'values' || prop === 'entries' || prop === Symbol.iterator) {\n        return (...args: any[]) => {\n          if (collector.active) {\n            resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n            for (const key of map.keys()) {\n              addDependency(collector, `${path}.map:${dependencyKey(key)}`, map.get(key), 'state')\n            }\n          }\n          if (prop === Symbol.iterator) {\n            return map[Symbol.iterator]()\n          }\n          return (map as any)[prop](...args)\n        }\n      }\n      if (prop === 'forEach') {\n        return (callback: (value: any, key: any, map: Map<any, any>) => void, thisArg?: any) => {\n          if (collector.active) {\n            resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n            for (const key of map.keys()) {\n              addDependency(collector, `${path}.map:${dependencyKey(key)}`, map.get(key), 'state')\n            }\n          }\n          return map.forEach((value, key) => callback(value, key, map), thisArg)\n        }\n      }\n      if (prop === 'size') {\n        if (collector.active) {\n          resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n          addDependency(collector, path, map.size, 'state')\n        }\n        return map.size\n      }\n      const value = Reflect.get(map, prop, receiver)\n      return typeof value === 'function' ? value.bind(map) : value\n    },\n    has(map, prop) {\n      if (collector.active) {\n        resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n        addDependency(collector, path, true, 'state')\n      }\n      return Reflect.has(map, prop)\n    },\n    ownKeys(map) {\n      if (collector.active) {\n        resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n        addDependency(collector, path, true, 'state')\n      }\n      return Reflect.ownKeys(map)\n    },\n  })\n  trackedProxyTargets.set(proxy, target)\n  return proxy\n}\n\nfunction createTrackedSet(target: Set<any>, path: string, collector: Collector, pendingPath: string): any {\n  const recordValue = (value: any, membership?: boolean) => {\n    const valuePath = `${path}.set:${dependencyKey(value)}`\n    if (collector.active) {\n      resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n      addDependency(collector, valuePath, typeof membership === 'undefined' ? target.has(value) : membership, 'state')\n    }\n    return valuePath\n  }\n\n  const proxy = new Proxy(target, {\n    get(set, prop, receiver) {\n      if (prop === 'has') {\n        return (value: any) => {\n          recordValue(value, set.has(value))\n          return set.has(value)\n        }\n      }\n      if (prop === 'keys' || prop === 'values' || prop === 'entries' || prop === Symbol.iterator) {\n        return (...args: any[]) => {\n          if (collector.active) {\n            resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n            for (const value of set.values()) {\n              addDependency(collector, `${path}.set:${dependencyKey(value)}`, true, 'state')\n            }\n          }\n          if (prop === Symbol.iterator) {\n            return set[Symbol.iterator]()\n          }\n          return (set as any)[prop](...args)\n        }\n      }\n      if (prop === 'forEach') {\n        return (callback: (value: any, value2: any, set: Set<any>) => void, thisArg?: any) => {\n          if (collector.active) {\n            resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n            for (const value of set.values()) {\n              addDependency(collector, `${path}.set:${dependencyKey(value)}`, true, 'state')\n            }\n          }\n          return set.forEach((value) => callback(value, value, set), thisArg)\n        }\n      }\n      if (prop === 'size') {\n        if (collector.active) {\n          resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n          addDependency(collector, path, set.size, 'state')\n        }\n        return set.size\n      }\n      const value = Reflect.get(set, prop, receiver)\n      return typeof value === 'function' ? value.bind(set) : value\n    },\n    has(set, prop) {\n      if (collector.active) {\n        resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n        addDependency(collector, path, true, 'state')\n      }\n      return Reflect.has(set, prop)\n    },\n    ownKeys(set) {\n      if (collector.active) {\n        resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n        addDependency(collector, path, true, 'state')\n      }\n      return Reflect.ownKeys(set)\n    },\n  })\n  trackedProxyTargets.set(proxy, target)\n  return proxy\n}\n\nfunction createTrackedObject(target: any, path: string, collector: Collector, pendingPath: string): any {\n  const proxy: any = new Proxy(target, {\n    get(object, prop, receiver) {\n      const value = Reflect.get(object, prop, receiver)\n\n      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {\n        if (prop === Symbol.iterator) {\n          return value\n        }\n        return value\n      }\n\n      // Methods that compare elements by reference must run against the raw array,\n      // otherwise the proxies we return for nested elements would break `includes`,\n      // `indexOf` and `lastIndexOf`. Track the indices they inspected manually.\n      if (Array.isArray(object) && (prop === 'includes' || prop === 'indexOf' || prop === 'lastIndexOf')) {\n        return (...args: any[]) => {\n          const methodName = prop as 'includes' | 'indexOf' | 'lastIndexOf'\n          const result = (Array.prototype as any)[methodName].apply(object, args)\n          if (collector.active) {\n            const length = object.length\n            let from = methodName === 'lastIndexOf' ? length - 1 : 0\n            if (args.length > 1 && typeof args[1] === 'number') {\n              from =\n                args[1] < 0\n                  ? methodName === 'lastIndexOf'\n                    ? length + args[1]\n                    : Math.max(length + args[1], 0)\n                  : Math.min(args[1], methodName === 'lastIndexOf' ? length - 1 : length)\n            }\n            let stop: number\n            if (methodName === 'includes') {\n              const found = object.indexOf(args[0], Math.max(from, 0))\n              stop = found >= 0 ? found : length - 1\n            } else if (typeof result === 'number' && result >= 0) {\n              stop = result\n            } else {\n              stop = methodName === 'lastIndexOf' ? 0 : length - 1\n            }\n            resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n            if (methodName === 'lastIndexOf') {\n              for (let i = from; i >= stop; i--) {\n                if (i >= 0 && i < length) {\n                  addDependency(collector, childPath(path, String(i)), object[i], 'state')\n                }\n              }\n            } else {\n              for (let i = from; i <= stop; i++) {\n                if (i >= 0 && i < length) {\n                  addDependency(collector, childPath(path, String(i)), object[i], 'state')\n                }\n              }\n            }\n          }\n          return result\n        }\n      }\n\n      // Other array methods are invoked with the proxy as `this`, so their reads of individual\n      // indices are tracked. `length` is deliberately ignored to keep dependency lists\n      // focused on the elements that were actually inspected.\n      if (typeof value === 'function') {\n        return value.bind(proxy)\n      }\n      if (Array.isArray(object) && prop === 'length') {\n        if (collector.active) {\n          resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n          addDependency(collector, childPath(path, 'length'), value, 'state')\n        }\n        return value\n      }\n\n      if (!collector.active) {\n        return value\n      }\n\n      resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n      const pathToChild = childPath(path, prop)\n\n      if (isTrackable(value)) {\n        addPendingDependency(collector, pathToChild, value, 'state')\n        return wrapTrackedValue(value, pathToChild, collector, pathToChild)\n      }\n\n      addDependency(collector, pathToChild, value, 'state')\n      return value\n    },\n    has(object, prop) {\n      if (collector.active) {\n        resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n        addDependency(collector, path, true, 'state')\n      }\n      return Reflect.has(object, prop)\n    },\n    ownKeys(object) {\n      if (collector.active) {\n        resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n        addDependency(collector, path, true, 'state')\n      }\n      return Reflect.ownKeys(object)\n    },\n  })\n  trackedProxyTargets.set(proxy, target)\n  return proxy\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Selector creation\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\n/** Registers a placeholder selector (used while other selectors are still being defined). */\nexport function registerAtomicSelectorPlaceholder(logic: Logic, name: string, selector: Selector): void {\n  const state = getStateForLogic(logic)\n  const node = getOrCreateNode(state, name, 'derived')\n  selectorMeta.set(selector, { state, node })\n}\n\n/** Creates a selector that reads a reducer key from the logic's slice of the store. */\nexport function createAtomicBaseSelector(logic: Logic, name: string, read: (state: any, props: any) => any): Selector {\n  const state = getStateForLogic(logic)\n  const node = getOrCreateNode(state, name, 'base')\n  node.read = read\n  node.dependencies.set(name, { path: name, kind: 'base', value: undefined })\n\n  const selector: Selector = function (stateArg?: any, propsArg?: any) {\n    const storeState = typeof stateArg === 'undefined' ? getStoreState() : stateArg\n    const props = typeof propsArg === 'undefined' ? logic.props : propsArg\n    const value = read(storeState, props)\n\n    if (!node.hasValue || !Object.is(node.value, value)) {\n      node.evaluations += 1\n      node.value = value\n      node.hasValue = true\n    }\n    node.dirty = false\n    node.stateInvalidated = false\n\n    if (activeCollector && activeCollector.active && activeCollector.node !== node) {\n      const collector = activeCollector\n      if (collector.node.state === state) {\n        addPendingDependency(collector, name, value, 'state')\n        if (isTrackable(value)) {\n          return wrapTrackedValue(value, name, collector, name)\n        }\n      } else {\n        addDependency(collector, foreignDependencyName(node), value, 'selector')\n        linkCrossDependency(node, collector.node)\n      }\n    }\n\n    return value\n  }\n\n  selectorMeta.set(selector, { state, node })\n  return selector\n}\n\n/** Creates a derived selector (the result of the `selectors({})` builder). */\nexport function createAtomicDerivedSelector(\n  logic: Logic,\n  name: string,\n  inputSelectors: Selector[],\n  computeFn: (...args: any[]) => any,\n  memoizeOptions?: Record<string, any>,\n): Selector {\n  const state = getStateForLogic(logic)\n  const node = getOrCreateNode(state, name, 'derived')\n\n  node.inputSelectors = inputSelectors\n  node.inputNodes = []\n  node.staticInputNames = []\n  node.computeFn = computeFn\n  node.memoizeOptions = memoizeOptions\n\n  for (const inputSelector of inputSelectors) {\n    const meta = selectorMeta.get(inputSelector)\n    if (meta && meta.state === state) {\n      node.inputNodes.push(meta.node)\n      node.staticInputNames.push(meta.node.name)\n      meta.node.dependents.add(name)\n    } else {\n      node.inputNodes.push(undefined)\n    }\n  }\n\n  const selector: Selector = function (stateArg?: any, propsArg?: any) {\n    return evaluateNode(node, stateArg, propsArg)\n  }\n\n  node.selector = selector\n  selectorMeta.set(selector, { state, node })\n  return selector\n}\n\nfunction propsDiffer(previous: any, next: any): boolean {\n  if (previous === next) {\n    return false\n  }\n  if (!previous || !next) {\n    return previous !== next\n  }\n  const previousKeys = Object.keys(previous)\n  const nextKeys = Object.keys(next)\n  if (previousKeys.length !== nextKeys.length) {\n    return true\n  }\n  for (const key of previousKeys) {\n    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(next, key) || previous[key] !== next[key]) {\n      return true\n    }\n  }\n  return false\n}\n\nfunction recordSelectorDependency(collector: Collector | null, node: AtomicNode, value: any): void {\n  if (!collector || !collector.active || collector.node === node) {\n    return\n  }\n  if (collector.node.state !== node.state) {\n    addDependency(collector, foreignDependencyName(node), value, 'selector')\n    linkCrossDependency(node, collector.node)\n    return\n  }\n  addDependency(collector, node.name, value, 'selector')\n  node.dependents.add(collector.node.name)\n}\n\nfunction evaluateNode(node: AtomicNode, stateArg?: any, propsArg?: any): any {\n  const logic = node.state.logic\n  const storeState = typeof stateArg === 'undefined' ? getStoreState() : stateArg\n  const props = typeof propsArg === 'undefined' ? logic.props : propsArg\n  const outerCollector = activeCollector\n\n  if (node.evaluating) {\n    throw new Error(CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR)\n  }\n\n  const propsChanged = node.hasValue && propsDiffer(node.lastProps, props)\n\n  if (node.hasValue && !node.dirty && !propsChanged) {\n    recordSelectorDependency(outerCollector, node, node.value)\n    return node.value\n  }\n\n  const collector = createCollector(node)\n  const wasStateInvalidated = !node.hasValue || node.stateInvalidated || propsChanged\n\n  node.evaluating = true\n  activeCollector = collector\n\n  let result: any\n  try {\n    const inputValues: any[] = []\n    for (let i = 0; i < node.inputSelectors.length; i++) {\n      inputValues.push(node.inputSelectors[i](storeState, props))\n    }\n\n    let inputChanged = false\n    if (node.hasValue) {\n      for (let i = 0; i < inputValues.length; i++) {\n        if (Object.is(inputValues[i], node.lastInputs[i])) {\n          continue\n        }\n        const inputNode = node.inputNodes[i]\n        // Base selector input references can change merely because an unrelated property in\n        // the same reducer changed. That case is handled precisely by the tracked leaf\n        // dependencies, so only derived and opaque inputs force a re-evaluation here.\n        if (!inputNode || inputNode.kind === 'derived') {\n          inputChanged = true\n          break\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (node.hasValue && !wasStateInvalidated && !inputChanged) {\n      node.dirty = false\n      node.stateInvalidated = false\n      node.lastInputs = inputValues\n      node.lastProps = props ? { ...props } : props\n      collector.active = false\n      result = node.value\n    } else {\n      const computed = unwrapTrackedValue(node.computeFn!(...inputValues))\n      node.evaluations += 1\n\n      let finalValue = computed\n      const resultEqualityCheck = node.memoizeOptions && node.memoizeOptions.resultEqualityCheck\n      if (node.hasValue && typeof resultEqualityCheck === 'function' && resultEqualityCheck(node.value, computed)) {\n        finalValue = node.value\n      }\n\n      node.value = finalValue\n      node.hasValue = true\n      node.lastInputs = inputValues\n      node.lastProps = props ? { ...props } : props\n      node.dirty = false\n      node.stateInvalidated = false\n      node.dependencies = finalizeCollector(collector)\n      result = finalValue\n    }\n  } finally {\n    collector.active = false\n    activeCollector = outerCollector\n    node.evaluating = false\n  }\n\n  recordSelectorDependency(outerCollector, node, result)\n  return result\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * State diffing & invalidation\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\nfunction isPlainObject(value: any): boolean {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') {\n    return false\n  }\n  const proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(value)\n  return proto === Object.prototype || proto === null\n}\n\nfunction pathJoin(path: string, key: string): string {\n  return path ? `${path}.${key}` : key\n}\n\nfunction diffLeaves(previous: any, next: any, path: string, changed: Set<string>): void {\n  if (Object.is(previous, next)) {\n    return\n  }\n\n  const previousIsMap = previous instanceof Map\n  const nextIsMap = next instanceof Map\n  if (previousIsMap || nextIsMap) {\n    if (!previousIsMap || !nextIsMap) {\n      changed.add(path)\n      return\n    }\n    diffMaps(previous, next, path, changed)\n    return\n  }\n\n  const previousIsSet = previous instanceof Set\n  const nextIsSet = next instanceof Set\n  if (previousIsSet || nextIsSet) {\n    if (!previousIsSet || !nextIsSet) {\n      changed.add(path)\n      return\n    }\n    diffSets(previous, next, path, changed)\n    return\n  }\n\n  const previousIsArray = Array.isArray(previous)\n  const nextIsArray = Array.isArray(next)\n  if (previousIsArray || nextIsArray) {\n    if (!previousIsArray || !nextIsArray) {\n      changed.add(path)\n      return\n    }\n    if (previous.length !== next.length) {\n      changed.add(pathJoin(path, 'length'))\n    }\n    const length = Math.max(previous.length, next.length)\n    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {\n      const indexPath = pathJoin(path, String(i))\n      if (i >= previous.length || i >= next.length) {\n        changed.add(indexPath)\n      } else {\n        diffLeaves(previous[i], next[i], indexPath, changed)\n      }\n    }\n    return\n  }\n\n  const previousIsObject = isPlainObject(previous)\n  const nextIsObject = isPlainObject(next)\n  if (!previousIsObject || !nextIsObject) {\n    changed.add(path)\n    return\n  }\n\n  const keys = new Set<string>([...Object.keys(previous), ...Object.keys(next)])\n  for (const key of keys) {\n    const keyPath = pathJoin(path, key)\n    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(previous, key) || !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(next, key)) {\n      changed.add(keyPath)\n    } else {\n      diffLeaves(previous[key], next[key], keyPath, changed)\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction diffMaps(previous: Map<any, any>, next: Map<any, any>, path: string, changed: Set<string>): void {\n  const keys = new Set<any>([...previous.keys(), ...next.keys()])\n  for (const key of keys) {\n    const keyPath = `${path}.map:${dependencyKey(key)}`\n    if (!previous.has(key) || !next.has(key) || !Object.is(previous.get(key), next.get(key))) {\n      changed.add(keyPath)\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction diffSets(previous: Set<any>, next: Set<any>, path: string, changed: Set<string>): void {\n  const values = new Set<any>([...previous.values(), ...next.values()])\n  for (const value of values) {\n    if (previous.has(value) !== next.has(value)) {\n      changed.add(`${path}.set:${dependencyKey(value)}`)\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction pathMatchesDependency(dependency: string, changedPath: string): boolean {\n  if (dependency === changedPath) {\n    return true\n  }\n  if (changedPath.startsWith(`${dependency}.`)) {\n    return true\n  }\n  if (dependency.startsWith(`${changedPath}.`)) {\n    return true\n  }\n  // Map/Set dependencies use the `map:` and `set:` suffixes. If the whole collection was\n  // replaced, the diff reports the collection's own path, which should invalidate entries.\n  if (changedPath === dependency || When the\n  redux state changes we diff the logic's slice of the store and only invalidate the selectors whose\n  dependencies actually changed. This makes multiple changes in a single action collapse into a single re-evaluation.\n*/\n\ntype NodeKind = 'base' | 'derived'\ntype DependencyKind = 'state' | 'base' | 'selector'\n\ninterface Dependency {\n  path: string\n  kind: DependencyKind\n  value: any\n}\n\ninterface AtomicNode {\n  name: string\n  id: string\n  kind: NodeKind\n  state: AtomicLogicState\n\n  // base selectors\n  read?: (state: any, props: any) => any\n\n  // derived selectors\n  inputSelectors: Selector[]\n  inputNodes: (AtomicNode | undefined)[]\n  staticInputNames: string[]\n  computeFn?: (...args: any[]) => any\n  memoizeOptions?: Record<string, any>\n  selector?: Selector\n\n  dependencies: Map<string, Dependency>\n  dependents: Set<string>\n\n  evaluations: number\n  dirtyCause: string | null\n  dirty: boolean\n  stateInvalidated: boolean\n  evaluating: boolean\n  hasValue: boolean\n  value: any\n  lastInputs: any[]\n  lastProps: any\n}\n\ninterface AtomicLogicState {\n  logic: Logic\n  pathString: string\n  nodes: Map<string, AtomicNode>\n  order: string[]\n}\n\ninterface Collector {\n  node: AtomicNode\n  active: boolean\n  deps: Map<string, Dependency>\n  pending: Map<string, Dependency>\n}\n\nlet logicStates = new WeakMap<Logic, AtomicLogicState>()\nlet logicStatesByPath = new Map<string, AtomicLogicState>()\nlet selectorMeta = new WeakMap<Function, { state: AtomicLogicState; node: AtomicNode }>()\nlet crossDependents = new Map<AtomicNode, Set<AtomicNode>>()\nlet trackedProxyTargets = new WeakMap<object, any>()\nlet activeCollector: Collector | null = null\n\nexport const CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR = '[KEA] Circular dependency detected'\n\n/** Clears all engine state. Called when a new Kea context is opened. */\nexport function resetAtomicEngine(): void {\n  logicStates = new WeakMap()\n  logicStatesByPath = new Map()\n  selectorMeta = new WeakMap()\n  crossDependents = new Map()\n  trackedProxyTargets = new WeakMap()\n  activeCollector = null\n}\n\nfunction linkCrossDependency(from: AtomicNode, to: AtomicNode): void {\n  let dependents = crossDependents.get(from)\n  if (!dependents) {\n    dependents = new Set()\n    crossDependents.set(from, dependents)\n  }\n  dependents.add(to)\n}\n\nfunction foreignDependencyName(node: AtomicNode): string {\n  return `${node.state.pathString}::${node.name}`\n}\n\nexport function isAtomicSelectorsEnabled(): boolean {\n  const context = getContext()\n  return !!context && !!context.options && context.options.atomicSelectors === true\n}\n\nfunction getStateForLogic(logic: Logic): AtomicLogicState {\n  let state = logicStates.get(logic)\n  if (!state) {\n    state = {\n      logic,\n      pathString: logic.pathString,\n      nodes: new Map(),\n      order: [],\n    }\n    logicStates.set(logic, state)\n    logicStatesByPath.set(logic.pathString, state)\n  } else {\n    // The path may have been changed by a later build step. Keep the path index fresh.\n    state.pathString = logic.pathString\n    logicStatesByPath.set(logic.pathString, state)\n  }\n  return state\n}\n\nfunction getOrCreateNode(state: AtomicLogicState, name: string, kind: NodeKind): AtomicNode {\n  let node = state.nodes.get(name)\n  if (!node) {\n    node = {\n      name,\n      id: `${state.pathString}::${name}`,\n      kind,\n      state,\n      inputSelectors: [],\n      inputNodes: [],\n      staticInputNames: [],\n      dependencies: new Map(),\n      dependents: new Set(),\n      evaluations: 0,\n      dirtyCause: null,\n      dirty: false,\n      stateInvalidated: false,\n      evaluating: false,\n      hasValue: false,\n      value: undefined,\n      lastInputs: [],\n      lastProps: undefined,\n    }\n    state.nodes.set(name, node)\n    state.order.push(name)\n  } else {\n    node.kind = kind\n    node.id = `${state.pathString}::${name}`\n  }\n  return node\n}\n\nfunction createCollector(node: AtomicNode): Collector {\n  return {\n    node,\n    active: true,\n    deps: new Map(),\n    pending: new Map(),\n  }\n}\n\nfunction finalizeCollector(collector: Collector): Map<string, Dependency> {\n  collector.active = false\n  for (const [path, dependency] of collector.pending) {\n    if (!collector.deps.has(path)) {\n      collector.deps.set(path, dependency)\n    }\n  }\n  collector.pending.clear()\n  return collector.deps\n}\n\nfunction addDependency(collector: Collector, path: string, value: any, kind: DependencyKind): void {\n  if (!collector.active) {\n    return\n  }\n  collector.pending.delete(path)\n  collector.deps.set(path, { path, kind, value })\n}\n\nfunction addPendingDependency(collector: Collector, path: string, value: any, kind: DependencyKind): void {\n  if (!collector.active || collector.deps.has(path) || collector.pending.has(path)) {\n    return\n  }\n  collector.pending.set(path, { path, kind, value })\n}\n\nfunction resolvePendingDependency(collector: Collector, path: string): void {\n  if (!collector.active) {\n    return\n  }\n  collector.pending.delete(path)\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Tracking proxies\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\nfunction isTrackable(value: any): boolean {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') {\n    return false\n  }\n  if (Array.isArray(value) || value instanceof Map || value instanceof Set) {\n    return true\n  }\n  const proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(value)\n  return proto === Object.prototype || proto === null\n}\n\nfunction dependencyKey(key: any): string {\n  if (typeof key === 'string') {\n    return key\n  }\n  if (typeof key === 'symbol') {\n    return String(key)\n  }\n  try {\n    return String(key)\n  } catch (e) {\n    return String(JSON.stringify(key))\n  }\n}\n\nfunction childPath(path: string, key: PropertyKey): string {\n  return path ? `${path}.${String(key)}` : String(key)\n}\n\nfunction unwrapTrackedValue(value: any): any {\n  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {\n    const target = trackedProxyTargets.get(value)\n    if (typeof target !== 'undefined') {\n      return target\n    }\n  }\n  return value\n}\n\nfunction wrapTrackedValue(value: any, path: string, collector: Collector, pendingPath: string): any {\n  if (!isTrackable(value)) {\n    return value\n  }\n  if (value instanceof Map) {\n    return createTrackedMap(value, path, collector, pendingPath)\n  }\n  if (value instanceof Set) {\n    return createTrackedSet(value, path, collector, pendingPath)\n  }\n  return createTrackedObject(value, path, collector, pendingPath)\n}\n\nfunction createTrackedMap(target: Map<any, any>, path: string, collector: Collector, pendingPath: string): any {\n  const recordKey = (key: any, depValue?: any) => {\n    const keyPath = `${path}.map:${dependencyKey(key)}`\n    if (collector.active) {\n      resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n      addDependency(collector, keyPath, typeof depValue === 'undefined' ? target.get(key) : depValue, 'state')\n    }\n    return keyPath\n  }\n\n  const proxy = new Proxy(target, {\n    get(map, prop, receiver) {\n      if (prop === 'get') {\n        return (key: any) => {\n          recordKey(key)\n          return map.get(key)\n        }\n      }\n      if (prop === 'has') {\n        return (key: any) => {\n          const keyPath = recordKey(key, map.has(key))\n          return map.has(key)\n        }\n      }\n      if (prop === 'keys' || prop === 'values' || prop === 'entries' || prop === Symbol.iterator) {\n        return (...args: any[]) => {\n          if (collector.active) {\n            resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n            for (const key of map.keys()) {\n              addDependency(collector, `${path}.map:${dependencyKey(key)}`, map.get(key), 'state')\n            }\n          }\n          if (prop === Symbol.iterator) {\n            return map[Symbol.iterator]()\n          }\n          return (map as any)[prop](...args)\n        }\n      }\n      if (prop === 'forEach') {\n        return (callback: (value: any, key: any, map: Map<any, any>) => void, thisArg?: any) => {\n          if (collector.active) {\n            resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n            for (const key of map.keys()) {\n              addDependency(collector, `${path}.map:${dependencyKey(key)}`, map.get(key), 'state')\n            }\n          }\n          return map.forEach((value, key) => callback(value, key, map), thisArg)\n        }\n      }\n      if (prop === 'size') {\n        if (collector.active) {\n          resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n          addDependency(collector, path, map.size, 'state')\n        }\n        return map.size\n      }\n      const value = Reflect.get(map, prop, receiver)\n      return typeof value === 'function' ? value.bind(map) : value\n    },\n    has(map, prop) {\n      if (collector.active) {\n        resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n        addDependency(collector, path, true, 'state')\n      }\n      return Reflect.has(map, prop)\n    },\n    ownKeys(map) {\n      if (collector.active) {\n        resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n        addDependency(collector, path, true, 'state')\n      }\n      return Reflect.ownKeys(map)\n    },\n  })\n  trackedProxyTargets.set(proxy, target)\n  return proxy\n}\n\nfunction createTrackedSet(target: Set<any>, path: string, collector: Collector, pendingPath: string): any {\n  const recordValue = (value: any, membership?: boolean) => {\n    const valuePath = `${path}.set:${dependencyKey(value)}`\n    if (collector.active) {\n      resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n      addDependency(collector, valuePath, typeof membership === 'undefined' ? target.has(value) : membership, 'state')\n    }\n    return valuePath\n  }\n\n  const proxy = new Proxy(target, {\n    get(set, prop, receiver) {\n      if (prop === 'has') {\n        return (value: any) => {\n          recordValue(value, set.has(value))\n          return set.has(value)\n        }\n      }\n      if (prop === 'keys' || prop === 'values' || prop === 'entries' || prop === Symbol.iterator) {\n        return (...args: any[]) => {\n          if (collector.active) {\n            resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n            for (const value of set.values()) {\n              addDependency(collector, `${path}.set:${dependencyKey(value)}`, true, 'state')\n            }\n          }\n          if (prop === Symbol.iterator) {\n            return set[Symbol.iterator]()\n          }\n          return (set as any)[prop](...args)\n        }\n      }\n      if (prop === 'forEach') {\n        return (callback: (value: any, value2: any, set: Set<any>) => void, thisArg?: any) => {\n          if (collector.active) {\n            resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n            for (const value of set.values()) {\n              addDependency(collector, `${path}.set:${dependencyKey(value)}`, true, 'state')\n            }\n          }\n          return set.forEach((value) => callback(value, value, set), thisArg)\n        }\n      }\n      if (prop === 'size') {\n        if (collector.active) {\n          resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n          addDependency(collector, path, set.size, 'state')\n        }\n        return set.size\n      }\n      const value = Reflect.get(set, prop, receiver)\n      return typeof value === 'function' ? value.bind(set) : value\n    },\n    has(set, prop) {\n      if (collector.active) {\n        resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n        addDependency(collector, path, true, 'state')\n      }\n      return Reflect.has(set, prop)\n    },\n    ownKeys(set) {\n      if (collector.active) {\n        resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n        addDependency(collector, path, true, 'state')\n      }\n      return Reflect.ownKeys(set)\n    },\n  })\n  trackedProxyTargets.set(proxy, target)\n  return proxy\n}\n\nfunction createTrackedObject(target: any, path: string, collector: Collector, pendingPath: string): any {\n  const proxy: any = new Proxy(target, {\n    get(object, prop, receiver) {\n      const value = Reflect.get(object, prop, receiver)\n\n      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {\n        if (prop === Symbol.iterator) {\n          return value\n        }\n        return value\n      }\n\n      // Methods that compare elements by reference must run against the raw array,\n      // otherwise the proxies we return for nested elements would break `includes`,\n      // `indexOf` and `lastIndexOf`. Track the indices they inspected manually.\n      if (Array.isArray(object) && (prop === 'includes' || prop === 'indexOf' || prop === 'lastIndexOf')) {\n        return (...args: any[]) => {\n          const methodName = prop as 'includes' | 'indexOf' | 'lastIndexOf'\n          const result = (Array.prototype as any)[methodName].apply(object, args)\n          if (collector.active) {\n            const length = object.length\n            let from = methodName === 'lastIndexOf' ? length - 1 : 0\n            if (args.length > 1 && typeof args[1] === 'number') {\n              from =\n                args[1] < 0\n                  ? methodName === 'lastIndexOf'\n                    ? length + args[1]\n                    : Math.max(length + args[1], 0)\n                  : Math.min(args[1], methodName === 'lastIndexOf' ? length - 1 : length)\n            }\n            let stop: number\n            if (methodName === 'includes') {\n              const found = object.indexOf(args[0], Math.max(from, 0))\n              stop = found >= 0 ? found : length - 1\n            } else if (typeof result === 'number' && result >= 0) {\n              stop = result\n            } else {\n              stop = methodName === 'lastIndexOf' ? 0 : length - 1\n            }\n            resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n            if (methodName === 'lastIndexOf') {\n              for (let i = from; i >= stop; i--) {\n                if (i >= 0 && i < length) {\n                  addDependency(collector, childPath(path, String(i)), object[i], 'state')\n                }\n              }\n            } else {\n              for (let i = from; i <= stop; i++) {\n                if (i >= 0 && i < length) {\n                  addDependency(collector, childPath(path, String(i)), object[i], 'state')\n                }\n              }\n            }\n          }\n          return result\n        }\n      }\n\n      // Other array methods are invoked with the proxy as `this`, so their reads of individual\n      // indices are tracked. `length` is deliberately ignored to keep dependency lists\n      // focused on the elements that were actually inspected.\n      if (typeof value === 'function') {\n        return value.bind(proxy)\n      }\n      if (Array.isArray(object) && prop === 'length') {\n        if (collector.active) {\n          resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n          addDependency(collector, childPath(path, 'length'), value, 'state')\n        }\n        return value\n      }\n\n      if (!collector.active) {\n        return value\n      }\n\n      resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n      const pathToChild = childPath(path, prop)\n\n      if (isTrackable(value)) {\n        addPendingDependency(collector, pathToChild, value, 'state')\n        return wrapTrackedValue(value, pathToChild, collector, pathToChild)\n      }\n\n      addDependency(collector, pathToChild, value, 'state')\n      return value\n    },\n    has(object, prop) {\n      if (collector.active) {\n        resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n        addDependency(collector, path, true, 'state')\n      }\n      return Reflect.has(object, prop)\n    },\n    ownKeys(object) {\n      if (collector.active) {\n        resolvePendingDependency(collector, pendingPath)\n        addDependency(collector, path, true, 'state')\n      }\n      return Reflect.ownKeys(object)\n    },\n  })\n  trackedProxyTargets.set(proxy, target)\n  return proxy\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Selector creation\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\n/** Registers a placeholder selector (used while other selectors are still being defined). */\nexport function registerAtomicSelectorPlaceholder(logic: Logic, name: string, selector: Selector): void {\n  const state = getStateForLogic(logic)\n  const node = getOrCreateNode(state, name, 'derived')\n  selectorMeta.set(selector, { state, node })\n}\n\n/** Creates a selector that reads a reducer key from the logic's slice of the store. */\nexport function createAtomicBaseSelector(logic: Logic, name: string, read: (state: any, props: any) => any): Selector {\n  const state = getStateForLogic(logic)\n  const node = getOrCreateNode(state, name, 'base')\n  node.read = read\n  node.dependencies.set(name, { path: name, kind: 'base', value: undefined })\n\n  const selector: Selector = function (stateArg?: any, propsArg?: any) {\n    const storeState = typeof stateArg === 'undefined' ? getStoreState() : stateArg\n    const props = typeof propsArg === 'undefined' ? logic.props : propsArg\n    const value = read(storeState, props)\n\n    if (!node.hasValue || !Object.is(node.value, value)) {\n      node.evaluations += 1\n      node.value = value\n      node.hasValue = true\n    }\n    node.dirty = false\n    node.stateInvalidated = false\n\n    if (activeCollector && activeCollector.active && activeCollector.node !== node) {\n      const collector = activeCollector\n      if (collector.node.state === state) {\n        addPendingDependency(collector, name, value, 'state')\n        if (isTrackable(value)) {\n          return wrapTrackedValue(value, name, collector, name)\n        }\n      } else {\n        addDependency(collector, foreignDependencyName(node), value, 'selector')\n        linkCrossDependency(node, collector.node)\n      }\n    }\n\n    return value\n  }\n\n  selectorMeta.set(selector, { state, node })\n  return selector\n}\n\n/** Creates a derived selector (the result of the `selectors({})` builder). */\nexport function createAtomicDerivedSelector(\n  logic: Logic,\n  name: string,\n  inputSelectors: Selector[],\n  computeFn: (...args: any[]) => any,\n  memoizeOptions?: Record<string, any>,\n): Selector {\n  const state = getStateForLogic(logic)\n  const node = getOrCreateNode(state, name, 'derived')\n\n  node.inputSelectors = inputSelectors\n  node.inputNodes = []\n  node.staticInputNames = []\n  node.computeFn = computeFn\n  node.memoizeOptions = memoizeOptions\n\n  for (const inputSelector of inputSelectors) {\n    const meta = selectorMeta.get(inputSelector)\n    if (meta && meta.state === state) {\n      node.inputNodes.push(meta.node)\n      node.staticInputNames.push(meta.node.name)\n      meta.node.dependents.add(name)\n    } else {\n      node.inputNodes.push(undefined)\n    }\n  }\n\n  const selector: Selector = function (stateArg?: any, propsArg?: any) {\n    return evaluateNode(node, stateArg, propsArg)\n  }\n\n  node.selector = selector\n  selectorMeta.set(selector, { state, node })\n  return selector\n}\n\nfunction propsDiffer(previous: any, next: any): boolean {\n  if (previous === next) {\n    return false\n  }\n  if (!previous || !next) {\n    return previous !== next\n  }\n  const previousKeys = Object.keys(previous)\n  const nextKeys = Object.keys(next)\n  if (previousKeys.length !== nextKeys.length) {\n    return true\n  }\n  for (const key of previousKeys) {\n    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(next, key) || previous[key] !== next[key]) {\n      return true\n    }\n  }\n  return false\n}\n\nfunction recordSelectorDependency(collector: Collector | null, node: AtomicNode, value: any): void {\n  if (!collector || !collector.active || collector.node === node) {\n    return\n  }\n  if (collector.node.state !== node.state) {\n    addDependency(collector, foreignDependencyName(node), value, 'selector')\n    linkCrossDependency(node, collector.node)\n    return\n  }\n  addDependency(collector, node.name, value, 'selector')\n  node.dependents.add(collector.node.name)\n}\n\nfunction evaluateNode(node: AtomicNode, stateArg?: any, propsArg?: any): any {\n  const logic = node.state.logic\n  const storeState = typeof stateArg === 'undefined' ? getStoreState() : stateArg\n  const props = typeof propsArg === 'undefined' ? logic.props : propsArg\n  const outerCollector = activeCollector\n\n  if (node.evaluating) {\n    throw new Error(CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR)\n  }\n\n  const propsChanged = node.hasValue && propsDiffer(node.lastProps, props)\n\n  if (node.hasValue && !node.dirty && !propsChanged) {\n    recordSelectorDependency(outerCollector, node, node.value)\n    return node.value\n  }\n\n  const collector = createCollector(node)\n  const wasStateInvalidated = !node.hasValue || node.stateInvalidated || propsChanged\n\n  node.evaluating = true\n  activeCollector = collector\n\n  let result: any\n  try {\n    const inputValues: any[] = []\n    for (let i = 0; i < node.inputSelectors.length; i++) {\n      inputValues.push(node.inputSelectors[i](storeState, props))\n    }\n\n    let inputChanged = false\n    if (node.hasValue) {\n      for (let i = 0; i < inputValues.length; i++) {\n        if (Object.is(inputValues[i], node.lastInputs[i])) {\n          continue\n        }\n        const inputNode = node.inputNodes[i]\n        // Base selector input references can change merely because an unrelated property in\n        // the same reducer changed. That case is handled precisely by the tracked leaf\n        // dependencies, so only derived and opaque inputs force a re-evaluation here.\n        if (!inputNode || inputNode.kind === 'derived') {\n          inputChanged = true\n          break\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (node.hasValue && !wasStateInvalidated && !inputChanged) {\n      node.dirty = false\n      node.stateInvalidated = false\n      node.lastInputs = inputValues\n      node.lastProps = props ? { ...props } : props\n      collector.active = false\n      result = node.value\n    } else {\n      const computed = unwrapTrackedValue(node.computeFn!(...inputValues))\n      node.evaluations += 1\n\n      let finalValue = computed\n      const resultEqualityCheck = node.memoizeOptions && node.memoizeOptions.resultEqualityCheck\n      if (node.hasValue && typeof resultEqualityCheck === 'function' && resultEqualityCheck(node.value, computed)) {\n        finalValue = node.value\n      }\n\n      node.value = finalValue\n      node.hasValue = true\n      node.lastInputs = inputValues\n      node.lastProps = props ? { ...props } : props\n      node.dirty = false\n      node.stateInvalidated = false\n      node.dependencies = finalizeCollector(collector)\n      result = finalValue\n    }\n  } finally {\n    collector.active = false\n    activeCollector = outerCollector\n    node.evaluating = false\n  }\n\n  recordSelectorDependency(outerCollector, node, result)\n  return result\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * State diffing & invalidation\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\nfunction isPlainObject(value: any): boolean {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') {\n    return false\n  }\n  const proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(value)\n  return proto === Object.prototype || proto === null\n}\n\nfunction pathJoin(path: string, key: string): string {\n  return path ? `${path}.${key}` : key\n}\n\nfunction diffLeaves(previous: any, next: any, path: string, changed: Set<string>): void {\n  if (Object.is(previous, next)) {\n    return\n  }\n\n  const previousIsMap = previous instanceof Map\n  const nextIsMap = next instanceof Map\n  if (previousIsMap || nextIsMap) {\n    if (!previousIsMap || !nextIsMap) {\n      changed.add(path)\n      return\n    }\n    diffMaps(previous, next, path, changed)\n    return\n  }\n\n  const previousIsSet = previous instanceof Set\n  const nextIsSet = next instanceof Set\n  if (previousIsSet || nextIsSet) {\n    if (!previousIsSet || !nextIsSet) {\n      changed.add(path)\n      return\n    }\n    diffSets(previous, next, path, changed)\n    return\n  }\n\n  const previousIsArray = Array.isArray(previous)\n  const nextIsArray = Array.isArray(next)\n  if (previousIsArray || nextIsArray) {\n    if (!previousIsArray || !nextIsArray) {\n      changed.add(path)\n      return\n    }\n    if (previous.length !== next.length) {\n      changed.add(pathJoin(path, 'length'))\n    }\n    const length = Math.max(previous.length, next.length)\n    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {\n      const indexPath = pathJoin(path, String(i))\n      if (i >= previous.length || i >= next.length) {\n        changed.add(indexPath)\n      } else {\n        diffLeaves(previous[i], next[i], indexPath, changed)\n      }\n    }\n    return\n  }\n\n  const previousIsObject = isPlainObject(previous)\n  const nextIsObject = isPlainObject(next)\n  if (!previousIsObject || !nextIsObject) {\n    changed.add(path)\n    return\n  }\n\n  const keys = new Set<string>([...Object.keys(previous), ...Object.keys(next)])\n  for (const key of keys) {\n    const keyPath = pathJoin(path, key)\n    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(previous, key) || !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(next, key)) {\n      changed.add(keyPath)\n    } else {\n      diffLeaves(previous[key], next[key], keyPath, changed)\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction diffMaps(previous: Map<any, any>, next: Map<any, any>, path: string, changed: Set<string>): void {\n  const keys = new Set<any>([...previous.keys(), ...next.keys()])\n  for (const key of keys) {\n    const keyPath = `${path}.map:${dependencyKey(key)}`\n    if (!previous.has(key) || !next.has(key) || !Object.is(previous.get(key), next.get(key))) {\n      changed.add(keyPath)\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction diffSets(previous: Set<any>, next: Set<any>, path: string, changed: Set<string>): void {\n  const values = new Set<any>([...previous.values(), ...next.values()])\n  for (const value of values) {\n    if (previous.has(value) !== next.has(value)) {\n      changed.add(`${path}.set:${dependencyKey(value)}`)\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction pathMatchesDependency(dependency: string, changedPath: string): boolean {\n  if (dependency === changedPath) {\n    return true\n  }\n  if (changedPath.startsWith(`${dependency}.`)) {\n    return true\n  }\n  if (dependency.startsWith(`${changedPath}.`)) {\n    return true\n  }\n  // Map/Set dependencies use the `map:` and `set:` suffixes.
